# Compensating Controls Report

## 1. Executive Summary

**408** threats analyzed | **70** Control Found | **171** Partial Control | **167** No Control Found

**Coverage**: 17.2% Found | 41.9% Partial | 40.9% Missing

**Risk Reduction**: 2273.6 inherent -> 1836.7 residual (**19.2%** reduction)

**Highest-Risk Unmitigated Finding**: S-3 — partner-feed — Composite 9.1 (Critical)

| Metric | Value |
|--------|-------|
| Analysis date | 2026-10-21 |
| Source file | `baseline/risk-scores.md` |
| Target codebase | `stub-codebase` |
| Schema version | 1.0 |

**Coverage Distribution:**

| Status | Count | Percentage |
|--------|-------|------------|
| Control Found | 70 | 17.2% |
| Partial Control | 171 | 41.9% |
| No Control Found | 167 | 40.9% |
| **Total** | **408** | **100%** |

> Analysis completed within the file-read budget; no directories were skipped.

## 2. Coverage Matrix

Threats grouped by residual severity (Critical first, then High, Medium, Low). Within each group, threats are sorted by residual score descending.

### Critical Residual Severity

| Threat ID | CF | Component | Threat | Inherent Score | Inherent Severity | Control Status | Residual Score | Residual Severity |
|-----------|----|-----------|--------|----------------|-------------------|----------------|----------------|-------------------|
| S-3 | — | partner-feed | A shared or long-lived webhook signature on partner-feed lets anyone who obtains one copy authenticate as every tenant that uses the same deployment profile. | 9.1 | Critical | No Control Found | 9.1 | Critical |

## 3. Control Details

Per-control evidence showing detected security controls with their location, code evidence and threat coverage, grouped by control category.

### Authentication

#### CTRL-01 — Signed token verification at the gateway

**Category**: Authentication | **Status**: Control Found | **Effectiveness**: Moderate

**Detected in**: `src/gateway/token_verify.py:7`

```python
def verify_token(raw, keys, audience):
    claims = decode_signed(raw, keys, algorithms=["ES256"])
    if claims["aud"] != audience or claims["exp"] < now():
        raise Rejected("token not valid for this service")
    return claims
```

#### CTRL-03 — Role check on every request

def require_role(role):
    def guard(request):
        if role not in directory.roles_for(request.principal):

    return guard

## 4. Recommendations

Actionable recommendations for threats classified as "No Control Found" or "Partial Control", sorted by composite risk score descending.

### Critical / High Risk Gaps

#### 1. S-3 — partner-feed (Composite: 9.1, Critical)

**Current Status**: No Control Found

**What to Implement**: Implement access control for partner-feed. Evaluate the original caller's entitlement for each object and action, and deny by default when the check cannot be made. Place the control where signed carrier manifests are first received, so that nothing downstream has to repeat the check. This addresses: A shared or long-lived webhook signature on partner-feed lets anyone who obtains one copy authenticate as every tenant that uses the same deployment profile. Acceptance check: a caller without the entitlement is denied for every object and action, including through indirect paths, and the denial is recorded. Ship to the internal environment first, compare behaviour with the previous release and promote after reviewing the first week of records.

**Where to Implement**: `stub-codebase/src/partner_feed/access_control.py` — a new module next to the code that handles signed carrier manifests, so the owners of that code review the control with it

**Reference Patterns**: Role and attribute checks at the service boundary, deny-by-default policy

**Effort Estimate**: High — a cross-cutting change that touches several components, including the tests that keep the control in place after later changes

---

#### 2. S-1 — client-portal (Composite: 8.5, High)

**Current Status**: Partial Control

**What to Implement**: Harden the existing access control control: evaluate the original caller's entitlement for each object and action, and deny by default when the check cannot be made on the paths of client-portal that it does not yet cover. The current implementation is recorded at src/gateway/token_verify.py:7 and covers the main request path. Extend it to the secondary paths and add a test that exercises each of them. This addresses: An attacker replays a captured session cookie against client-portal, which does not bind the credential to the session or channel it was issued for, so the replay is accepted as the original caller. Acceptance check: a caller without the entitlement is denied for every object and action, including through indirect paths, and the denial is recorded. Roll the change out behind a flag, watch error rates for one release and remove the flag once no regression is seen.

**Where to Implement**: `src/gateway/token_verify.py` — the existing control lives here and covers the main request path of client-portal, and the owners of that module already review changes to it

**Reference Patterns**: Role and attribute checks at the service boundary, deny-by-default policy

**Effort Estimate**: Medium — the foundation exists, so the work is to extend coverage, add tests and confirm the secondary paths behave the same way

---

#### 3. S-2 — partner-feed (Composite: 8.2, High)

**Current Status**: No Control Found

**What to Implement**: Implement access control for partner-feed. Evaluate the original caller's entitlement for each object and action, and deny by default when the check cannot be made. Place the control where signed carrier manifests are first received, so that nothing downstream has to repeat the check. This addresses: A compromised peer presents a forged service identity to partner-feed, and partner-feed accepts the claim without verifying the signature chain back to the issuing authority. Acceptance check: a caller without the entitlement is denied for every object and action, including through indirect paths, and the denial is recorded. Apply the change to the most exposed instances first, then to the rest, and keep the previous configuration available for a quick rollback.

**Where to Implement**: `stub-codebase/src/partner_feed/access_control.py` — a new module next to the code that handles signed carrier manifests, so the owners of that code review the control with it

**Reference Patterns**: Role and attribute checks at the service boundary, deny-by-default policy

**Effort Estimate**: High — a cross-cutting change that touches several components, including the tests that keep the control in place after later changes

#### 4. S-4 — edge-gateway (Composite: 8.1, High)

**Current Status**: Partial Control

**What to Implement**: Harden the existing authentication control: verify the caller's signed credential on every request to this component and bind it to the channel it arrived on on the paths of edge-gateway that it does not yet cover. The current implementation is recorded at src/gateway/token_verify.py:7 and covers the main request path. Extend it to the secondary paths and add a test that exercises each of them. This addresses: A shared or long-lived bearer token on edge-gateway lets anyone who obtains one copy authenticate as every tenant that uses the same deployment profile. Acceptance check: a request that carries a missing, expired or mismatched credential is rejected with a logged reason, and a valid request still succeeds. Coordinate the change with the owners of dependent services so that none of them relies on the behaviour being removed.

**Where to Implement**: `src/gateway/token_verify.py` — the existing control lives here and covers the main request path of edge-gateway, and the owners of that module already review changes to it

**Reference Patterns**: Short-lived signed tokens, mutual TLS, workload identity

**Effort Estimate**: Low — the foundation exists, so the work is to extend coverage, add tests and confirm the secondary paths behave the same way

---

#### 5. LLM-3 — edge-gateway (Composite: 8.1, High)

**Current Status**: Partial Control

**What to Implement**: Harden the existing input validation control: validate each structured input against a closed schema before use, and reject unknown fields and oversized values on the paths of edge-gateway that it does not yet cover. The current implementation is recorded at src/intake/schema.py:7 and covers the main request path. Extend it to the secondary paths and add a test that exercises each of them. This addresses: An attacker splits a payload across several messages so that no single message trips the filter in front of edge-gateway. Acceptance check: malformed, oversized and unexpected fields are rejected before any side effect, and a regression test covers each rejected shape. Apply the change to the most exposed instances first, then to the rest, and keep the previous configuration available for a quick rollback.

**Where to Implement**: `src/intake/schema.py` — the existing control lives here and covers the main request path of edge-gateway, and the owners of that module already review changes to it

Apply the change to the most exposed instances first, then to the rest, and keep the previous configuration available for a quick rollback.

## 5. Residual Risk Summary

Comparison of inherent risk (before controls) to residual risk (after controls).

### Aggregate Risk Reduction

| Metric | Value |
|--------|-------|
| Total Inherent Risk Score | 2273.6 |
| Total Residual Risk Score | 1836.7 |
| Delta | 436.9 |
| Overall Reduction | 19.2% |

### Per-Severity-Band Shift

| Shift | Count | Examples |
|-------|-------|----------|
| Critical -> High | 0 | — |
| Critical -> Medium | 0 | — |
| Critical -> Low | 0 | — |
| High -> Medium | 21 | S-1, S-4, S-40 |
| High -> Low | 6 | S-5, D-2, D-3 |
| Medium -> Low | 103 | S-9, S-12, S-14 |
| No Shift | 278 | S-2, S-3, S-6 |
| **Total** | **408** |  |

### Severity Distribution Comparison

| Severity | Inherent Count | Residual Count | Change |
|----------|----------------|----------------|--------|
| Critical | 1 | 1 | +0 |
| High | 38 | 11 | -27 |
| Medium | 336 | 254 | -82 |
| Low | 33 | 142 | +109 |
| **Total** | **408** | **408** |  |

### Reduction Factor Reference

| Control Status | Reduction Factor | Formula | Description |
|----------------|------------------|---------|-------------|
| Control Found | 0.50 | Inherent * 0.50 | Control detected with evidence. Residual is 50% of inherent. |
| Partial Control | 0.25 | Inherent * 0.75 | Control exists but incomplete coverage. Residual is 75% of inherent. |
| No Control Found | 0.00 | Inherent * 1.00 | No matching control detected. Residual equals inherent. |

---

## 6. Methodology

This section documents the compensating controls analysis methodology used to produce this report.

### 6.1 Control Detection

The analysis scans the target codebase for security controls across 8 categories:

| Category | What It Detects | STRIDE Mapping |
|----------|-----------------|----------------|
| **Authentication** | Login mechanisms, token validation, session management, identity verification | Spoofing |
| **Access Control** | Role checks, permission guards, authorization middleware | Spoofing, Elevation of Privilege |
| **Input Validation** | Schema validation, sanitization, parameterized queries, type checking | Tampering |
| **Encryption** | TLS configuration, data-at-rest encryption, hashing, key management | Information Disclosure |
| **Rate Limiting** | Request throttling, circuit breakers, backpressure, quota enforcement | Denial of Service |
| **Logging/Audit** | Structured logging, audit trails, immutable logs, event tracking | Repudiation |
| **CSRF Protection** | Anti-CSRF tokens, SameSite cookies, origin validation | Tampering |
| **CSP/Security Headers** | Content-Security-Policy, HSTS, X-Frame-Options, security header middleware | Information Disclosure |

### 6.2 Classification Logic

| Classification | Criteria | Reduction Factor |
|----------------|----------|------------------|
| **Control Found** | A matching control is detected with file:line evidence that addresses the threat's attack vector | 0.50 |
| **Partial Control** | A control exists but does not cover all paths, vectors, or components targeted by the threat | 0.25 |
| **No Control Found** | No matching control detected in the target codebase for this threat | 0.00 |

When multiple controls address the same threat, the highest single control effectiveness is used (not additive).

### 6.3 Residual Risk Calculation

```
Residual Score = Inherent Score * (1 - Reduction Factor)
```

Residual scores are clamped to [0.0, 10.0], rounded half-up to one decimal place and mapped to severity bands with the thresholds used for risk scoring.

### 6.4 Data Sources

- **Scored threats**: parsed from `risk-scores.md` (canonical) or `risk-scores.sarif` (fallback).
- **Target codebase**: scanned through the `--target` path, with heuristic directories prioritized when no architecture mapping exists.
- **STRIDE-to-control mapping**: the canonical mapping from threat categories to control categories drives which controls are searched for.

### 6.5 Limitations

- Static analysis only: runtime control behavior is not evaluated.
- 200-file read budget: large codebases may have directories skipped with warnings.
- Files over 5,000 tokens are truncated to security-relevant sections.
- Binary reduction factors approximate control impact.
